# Python binding coverage of the sidereon public API

Core revision: `e2fb3dfdc392d23087ed8aa1ee028a0056b4021b`.

Every item the `sidereon-core` and `sidereon` crates make reachable through a public path, with the Python symbol that exposes it, or `MISSING`. The list is generated by `scripts/coverage/` from the core source and this package's `src/`. `pubapi.py` walks each crate's module tree from `lib.rs` (public modules, `pub use` re-exports and globs, items outside `#[cfg(test)]`) and lists each item once under its shortest public path, with the inherent `pub fn` methods of each type. `bindmap.py` marks an item exposed where a `#[pyclass]` holds it or a `#[pyfunction]` or `#[pymethods]` body (directly or through a helper it calls) refers to it, and a method exposed where such a body calls it by name. The method match is by name, so a method that shares its name with another type's can read as exposed when only the other is.

## Counts

| | exposed | MISSING |
|---|---|---|
| items | 1577 | 1490 |
| methods of items | 937 | 829 |
